````dart
  group('unions', () {
    test('a discriminator picks the variant and is written back', () {
      final pet = emit(r'''
    Root:
      oneOf:
        - $ref: '#/components/schemas/Cat'
        - $ref: '#/components/schemas/Dog'
      discriminator:
        propertyName: kind
        mapping: {cat: Cat, dog: Dog, puppy: Dog}
    Cat:
      type: object
      properties: {kind: {const: cat}}
    Dog:
      type: object
      properties: {bark: {type: boolean}}
''')['models/root.dart']!;
      for (final line in [
        "final r = JsonReader.of(json, schema: 'Root');",
        "return switch (r.json['kind']) {",
        "'cat' => RootCat(Cat.fromJson(JsonReader.asObject(r.json))),",
        "'dog' || 'puppy' => RootDog(Dog.fromJson(JsonReader.asObject(r.json))),",
        '_ => RootUnknown._(JsonValue(r.json)),',
        'Object? toJson() => value.toJson();',
        "Object? toJson() => {...value.toJson(), 'kind': 'dog'};",
        'final class RootUnknown implements Root {',
      ]) {
        expect(pet, contains(line));
      }
    });

    test('JSON types pick the variant, int before num (R9.3)', () {
      final id = emit('''
    Root:
      oneOf:
        - {type: number}
        - {type: string, format: date}
        - {type: integer}
        - {type: array, items: {type: string}}
''')['models/root.dart']!;
      expect(
        id,
        contains(
          '  factory Root.fromJson(Object? json) => switch (json) {\n'
          '    final String value => RootDate(JsonReader.asDate(value)),\n'
          '    final int value => RootInt(JsonReader.asInt(value)),\n'
          '    final List<Object?> value => '
          'RootList(JsonReader.listOf(JsonReader.asString)(value)),\n'
          '    final num value => RootNum(JsonReader.asNumber(value)),\n'
          '    _ => RootUnknown._(JsonValue(json)),\n'
          '  };\n',
        ),
      );
      expect(id, contains('const DeepEquality().equals(other.value, value)'));
    });

    test('union: structural tries the variants', () {
      const shapes = '''
    Root:
      KIND:
        - {type: object, required: [r], properties: {r: {type: number}}}
        - {type: object, required: [w], properties: {w: {type: number}}}
''';
      const structural = GenerationConfig(union: UnionMode.structural);
      final oneOf = emit(
        shapes.replaceFirst('KIND', 'oneOf'),
        generation: structural,
      )['models/root.dart']!;
      expect(oneOf, contains('final decoders = <Root Function()>['));
      expect(
        oneOf,
        contains(
          'return matches.length == 1 ? matches.single : '
          'RootUnknown._(JsonValue(json));',
        ),
      );
      final anyOf = emit(
        shapes.replaceFirst('KIND', 'anyOf'),
        generation: structural,
      )['models/root.dart']!;
      expect(
        anyOf,
        contains(
          'if (JsonReader.attempt(decode) case final value?) return value;',
        ),
      );
    });

    test('a union of unions wraps the inner union', () {
      final files = emit(r'''
    Root:
      oneOf:
        - $ref: '#/components/schemas/Inner'
        - {type: boolean}
    Inner:
      oneOf:
        - {type: object, properties: {kind: {const: a}}}
        - {type: object, properties: {kind: {const: b}}}
      discriminator: {propertyName: kind}
''');
      final root = files['models/root.dart']!;
      expect(
        root,
        contains(
          'final Map<String, Object?> value => RootInner(Inner.fromJson(value)),',
        ),
      );
      expect(files['models/inner.dart'], contains("'a' => InnerVariant1Case("));
    });
  });
}
````

````dart
import '../ir/ir.dart';
import 'dart_code.dart';
import 'dart_writer.dart';
import 'model_emitter.dart';
import 'type_codec.dart';

/// Writes an [EnumDecl] as an open enum (decision 9 of M8, research §C.3):
/// a `sealed` class (an `abstract interface` one when the enum is not
/// exhaustive) with a static alias per value, a Dart `enum` of the known
/// values that implements it, and a class for a value the spec does not
/// list, which keeps the raw value (R8.1) and only `fromJson` creates.
final class EnumEmitter {
  /// Creates an emitter that writes with [_codec].
  const EnumEmitter(this._codec);

  final TypeCodec _codec;

  static const _code = DartCode();

  /// Writes [decl] to [out].
  void write(EnumDecl decl, DartWriter out) {
    final name = decl.name;
    final known = decl.knownName;
    final unknown = decl.unknownName;
    final (type, read) = switch (decl.kind) {
      .string => ('String', 'asString'),
      .integer => ('int', 'asInt'),
      .number => ('num', 'asNumber'),
      .boolean => ('bool', 'asBool'),
    };
    _codec.imports.fromRuntime('JsonReader');
    out.doc(decl.description);
    if (decl.deprecated) out.line(ModelEmitter.deprecation);
    final modifier = decl.exhaustive ? 'sealed' : 'abstract interface';
    out.block('$modifier class $name', () {
      out
        ..line('/// Decodes a [$name]; a value the spec does not list is a')
        ..line('/// [$unknown].')
        ..block('factory $name.fromJson(Object? json)', () {
          out.line('final value = JsonReader.$read(json);');
          out.block('for (final known in $known.values)', () {
            out.line('if (known.value == value) return known;');
          });
          out.line('return $unknown._(value);');
        });
      for (final value in decl.values) {
        out.line();
        _valueDoc(out, value);
        out.line('static const ${value.name} = $known.${value.name};');
      }
      out
        ..line()
        ..line('/// The value in JSON.')
        ..line('$type get value;')
        ..line()
        ..line('/// The value in JSON.')
        ..line('$type toJson();');
    });
    out
      ..line()
      ..line('/// The values of [$name] the spec lists.')
      ..block('enum $known implements $name', () {

          _valueDoc(out, value);
          final last = i == decl.values.length - 1;
          out.line(

          );

        out
          ..line()
          ..line('const $known(this.value);')
          ..line()
          ..line('@override')
          ..line('final $type value;')
          ..line()
          ..line('@override')
          ..line('$type toJson() => value;');
      });
    out
      ..line()
      ..line('/// A value of [$name] the spec does not list; only')
      ..line('/// [$name.fromJson] creates one.')
      ..block('final class $unknown implements $name', () {
        out
          ..line('const $unknown._(this.value);')
          ..line()
          ..line('@override')
          ..line('final $type value;')
          ..line()
          ..line('@override')
          ..line('$type toJson() => value;')
          ..line()
          ..line('@override')
          ..line('bool operator ==(Object other) =>')
          ..indented(
            () => out.indented(
              () => out.line('other is $unknown && other.value == value;'),
            ),
          )
          ..line()
          ..line('@override')
          ..line('int get hashCode => value.hashCode;')
          ..line()
          ..line('@override')
          ..line(
            "String toString() => '${unknown.replaceAll(r'$', r'\$')}"
            r"($value)';",
          );
      });
  }

  void _valueDoc(DartWriter out, EnumValueIr value) {
    out.doc(value.description ?? '`${_code.literal(value.value)}`');
  }
}
````

````dart
import 'dart:convert';

import '../ir/dart_type.dart';
import '../ir/ir.dart';
import 'dart_code.dart';
import 'dart_writer.dart';
import 'type_codec.dart';

/// Writes the class of a [ModelDecl] (decisions 3, 4, 8, 10–13 of M8): a
/// `final class` with a `const` constructor, `fromJson`, `toJson`, deep
/// `==` and `hashCode`, and `toString`.
///

/// known values in the constructor (R8.2); `fromJson`, which may meet
/// unknown ones, then uses a private constructor `_`.
final class ModelEmitter {
  /// Creates an emitter that writes with [_codec].
  const ModelEmitter(this._codec);

  final TypeCodec _codec;

  static const _code = DartCode();

  /// The message of `@Deprecated` for what the spec marks deprecated.
  static const deprecation = "@Deprecated('Deprecated in the API spec.')";

  /// Writes [model] to [out].
  void write(ModelDecl model, DartWriter out) {
    final name = model.name;
    final fields = [
      for (final field in model.fields)
        if (field.constValue == null) field,
    ];
    final constants = [
      for (final field in model.fields)
        if (field.constValue != null) field,
    ];
    final extra = model.additionalProperties;
    final known =
        model.direction != .response &&
        fields.any((f) => _codec.hasEnum(f.type));
    _codec.imports.fromRuntime('immutable');
    out.doc(model.description);
    if (model.deprecated) out.line(deprecation);
    out
      ..line('@immutable')
      ..block('final class $name', () {
        out.line('/// Creates a [$name].');
        _constructor(out, 'const $name', model, fields, typed: known);
        if (known) {
          out.line();
          _constructor(out, 'const $name._', model, fields, typed: false);
        }
        out.line();
        _fromJson(out, model, fields, constants, private: known);
        for (final field in constants) {
          out.line();
          _constant(out, field);
        }
        for (final field in fields) {
          out.line();
          _field(out, field);
        }
        if (extra != null) {
          out
            ..line()
            ..line(
              '/// The properties of the object the schema does not list '
              '(`additionalProperties`).',
            )
            ..line(
              'final ${_codec.type(MapType(extra))} '
              '${model.additionalPropertiesName};',
            );
        }
        out.line();
        _toJson(out, model, fields, constants);
        out.line();
        _equality(out, model, fields);
        out.line();
        _toString(out, model, fields);
      });

  void _constructor(
    DartWriter out,
    String head,
    ModelDecl model,
    List<FieldIr> fields, {
    required bool typed,
  }) {
    final params = [
      for (final field in fields) _parameter(field, typed: typed),
      if (model.additionalProperties != null)
        'this.${model.additionalPropertiesName} = const {}',
    ];
    if (params.isEmpty) {
      out.line('$head();');
    } else {
      out.list('$head({', params, '});');
    }

  String _parameter(FieldIr field, {required bool typed}) {
    final type = field.type;
    final declared = typed && _codec.hasEnum(type)
        ? '${_codec.known(type)} '
        : '';
    if (field.required) return 'required ${declared}this.${field.name}';
    if (_isJson(type)) {
      _codec.imports.fromRuntime('JsonValue');
      return '${declared}this.${field.name} = const JsonValue(null)';

  void _fromJson(

    ModelDecl model,
    List<FieldIr> fields,
    List<FieldIr> constants, {
    required bool private,
  }) {

    final extra = model.additionalProperties;

      out.line('$head => const $create();');

  void _field(DartWriter out, FieldIr field) {
    _docs(out, field);
    out.line('final ${_codec.type(field.type)} ${field.name};');
  }

  void _docs(DartWriter out, FieldIr field, {bool constant = false}) {
    final parts = [

      if (constant && field.description == null)
        'Always `${jsonEncode(field.constValue)}`.',
      ?_constraints(field.constraints),
      if (field.defaultValue case (final value,))

  /// The constraints of a field as a sentence (decision 12), such as

  static String? _constraints(ConstraintsIr c) {
    if (c.isEmpty) return null;
    String range(int? min, int? max, String unit) => switch ((min, max)) {
      (final a?, final b?) when a == b => '$a $unit',
      (final a?, final b?) => '$a–$b $unit',
      (final a?, null) => 'at least $a $unit',
      (null, final b?) => 'at most $b $unit',
      (null, null) => '',

    final pattern = c.pattern;
    final parts = [

      if (pattern != null && pattern.contains('`'))

        'matches `$pattern`',

      if (c.exclusiveMinimum case final v?) 'greater than $v',
      if (c.maximum case final v?) 'at most $v',
      if (c.exclusiveMaximum case final v?) 'less than $v',
      if (c.multipleOf case final v?) 'a multiple of $v',
      range(c.minItems, c.maxItems, 'items'),
      if (c.uniqueItems) 'unique items',
      range(c.minProperties, c.maxProperties, 'properties'),
    ].where((part) => part.isNotEmpty);
    final text = parts.join(', ');
    return '${text[0].toUpperCase()}${text.substring(1)}.';

  void _toJson(

      final name = model.additionalPropertiesName;

      out.list('Map<String, Object?> toJson() => {', entries, '};');

  String _entry(FieldIr field, String key) {

    final nullable = _codec.encodeNullable(type, name);
    if (nullable.startsWith('$name?.')) return '$key: ?$nullable';
    return 'if ($name case final v?) $key: ${_codec.encode(inner, 'v')}';

    final name = model.name;
````

````dart
import '../ir/dart_type.dart';
import '../ir/ir.dart';
import '../ir/names.dart';
import 'dart_writer.dart';
import 'enum_emitter.dart';
import 'imports.dart';
import 'model_emitter.dart';
import 'type_codec.dart';
import 'union_emitter.dart';

/// The model files of a client (decision 2 of M8): one file per declaration
/// in `models/`, named after the declaration in snake case, and the barrel
/// `models.dart` that exports them.
///
/// Files import each other by relative paths. Two declarations whose names
/// give the same file name, which also happens on file systems that ignore
/// case (`UserDTO`, `UserDto`), get `2`, `3`, ... in the order of their
/// names. Every file starts with [header].
final class ModelLibrary {
  /// Creates the library writer; lines are wrapped at [width].
  const ModelLibrary({this.width = 80});

  /// The line width.
  final int width;

  /// The first lines of every generated file.
  static const header =
      '// GENERATED CODE - DO NOT MODIFY BY HAND.\n// dart format off\n';

  /// The directory of the model files, relative to the output directory.
  static const directory = 'models';

  /// The barrel file, relative to the output directory.
  static const barrel = 'models.dart';

  static const _names = DartNames();

  /// The files of [ir] by path relative to the output directory, sorted by
  /// path.
  Map<String, String> files(ClientIr ir) {
    final fileNames = this.fileNames(ir);
    final files = <String, String>{};
    for (final declaration in ir.declarations) {
      final file = fileNames[declaration.id]!;
      final imports = Imports(file);
      final codec = TypeCodec(ir, imports, (id) => fileNames[id]!);
      final body = DartWriter(width);
      switch (declaration) {
        case final ModelDecl model:
          ModelEmitter(codec).write(model, body);
        case final EnumDecl decl:
          EnumEmitter(codec).write(decl, body);
        case final UnionDecl decl:
          UnionEmitter(codec).write(decl, body);
      }
      final out = DartWriter(width)
        ..line(header.trimRight())
        ..line();
      imports.write(out);
      files['$directory/$file'] = '${out.text}${body.text}';
    }
    final exports = DartWriter(width)
      ..line(header.trimRight())
      ..line();
    final paths = files.keys.toList()..sort();
    for (final path in paths) {
      exports.line("export '$path';");
    }
    files[barrel] = exports.text;
    return {for (final path in files.keys.toList()..sort()) path: files[path]!};
  }

  /// The file name of every declaration of [ir], without the directory.
  Map<DeclId, String> fileNames(ClientIr ir) {
    final scope = NameScope(reserved: const {}, reservedSuffix: '');
    return {
      for (final declaration in ir.declarations)
        declaration.id: '${scope.claim(_snake(declaration.name)).$1}.dart',
    };
  }

  /// [name] in lower snake case; a name that does not start with a letter
  /// gets `type_` in front, as Dart file names must.
  static String _snake(String name) {
    final snake = _names.words(name).map((w) => w.toLowerCase()).join('_');
    return snake.startsWith(RegExp('[a-z]')) ? snake : 'type_$snake';
  }
}
````

````dart
import '../ir/dart_type.dart';
import '../ir/ir.dart';
import 'imports.dart';

/// How generated code names, decodes, encodes and compares values of a
/// [DartType] in one file; every name it writes is imported into
/// [_imports].
final class TypeCodec {
  /// Creates the codec of a file of [_ir] with [_imports]; [_fileOf] gives
  /// the file that declares a declaration.
  TypeCodec(this._ir, this._imports, this._fileOf);

  final ClientIr _ir;
  final Imports _imports;
  final String Function(DeclId id) _fileOf;

  /// The imports of the file.
  Imports get imports => _imports;

  /// The declaration [id] names; every generated type has one.
  Declaration declaration(DeclId id) => _ir[id]!;

  /// [type] as Dart code.
  String type(DartType type) {
    _use(type);
    return type.code(_ir.nameOf);
  }

  /// [type] with the known values of every enum in it (`StatusKnown`
  /// for `Status`), for a constructor of a model sent in requests (R8.2).
  String known(DartType type) {
    final mark = type.nullable ? '?' : '';
    return switch (type) {
      NamedType(:final id) => switch (declaration(id)) {
        final EnumDecl e => _named(id, '${e.knownName}$mark'),
        _ => this.type(type),
      },
      ListType(:final item) => 'List<${known(item)}>$mark',
      MapType(:final value) => 'Map<String, ${known(value)}>$mark',
      PatchType(:final value) => _runtime(
        'Patch',
        'Patch<${known(value)}>$mark',
      ),
      _ => this.type(type),
    };
  }

  /// Whether [type] has an enum in it, so [known] differs from [type].
  bool hasEnum(DartType type) => switch (type) {
    NamedType(:final id) => declaration(id) is EnumDecl,
    ListType(:final item) => hasEnum(item),
    MapType(:final value) => hasEnum(value),
    PatchType(:final value) => hasEnum(value),
    _ => false,
  };

  /// A decoder of [type], `T Function(Object? value)`, for a value that
  /// is not `null` (a nullable item or map value is decoded by
  /// `JsonReader.nullable`).
  String decoder(DartType type) {
    _imports.fromRuntime('JsonReader');
    return switch (type) {
      CoreType(:final kind) => switch (kind) {
        .integer => 'JsonReader.asInt',
        .float => 'JsonReader.asDouble',
        .number => 'JsonReader.asNumber',
        .string => 'JsonReader.asString',
        .boolean => 'JsonReader.asBool',
        .dateTime => 'JsonReader.asDateTime',
        .date => 'JsonReader.asDate',
        .bytes || .binary => 'JsonReader.asBytes',
        .json => 'JsonReader.asValue',
      },
      ListType(:final item) => 'JsonReader.listOf(${_item(item)})',
      MapType(:final value) => 'JsonReader.mapOf(${_item(value)})',
      NamedType(:final id) => switch (declaration(id)) {
        ModelDecl(:final name) => _named(
          id,
          'JsonReader.objectOf($name.fromJson)',
        ),
        final other => _named(id, '${other.name}.fromJson'),
      },
      final CustomType custom => _custom(custom),
      PatchType(:final value) => decoder(value),
    };
  }

  String _item(DartType type) => type.nullable && !_isJson(type)
      ? 'JsonReader.nullable(${decoder(type)})'
      : decoder(type);

  String _custom(CustomType custom) {
    final CustomType(:type, :json) = custom;
    final converter = _converter(custom);
    final read = switch (json) {
      MapType(value: CoreType(kind: .json)) => 'JsonReader.asObject',
      ListType(item: CoreType(kind: .json)) => 'JsonReader.asList',
      CoreType(kind: .json) => '',
      _ => decoder(json),
    };
    final value = read.isEmpty ? 'value' : '$read(value)';
    return converter == null
        ? '(value) => $type.fromJson($value)'
        : '(value) => const $converter().fromJson($value)';
  }

  /// Whether a value of [type] is its own JSON (a scalar, `JsonValue`, or
  /// a list or map of them), so [encode] returns it as is.
  bool isIdentity(DartType type) => switch (type) {
    CoreType(:final kind) => switch (kind) {
      .integer || .float || .number || .string || .boolean || .json => true,
      _ => false,
    },
    ListType(:final item) => isIdentity(item),
    MapType(:final value) => isIdentity(value),
    _ => false,
  };

  /// The JSON of [value], an expression of [type] that is not `null`;
  /// [depth] keeps the names of nested loop variables apart.
  String encode(DartType type, String value, [int depth = 0]) {
    if (isIdentity(type)) return value;
    return switch (type) {
      CoreType(:final kind) => switch (kind) {
        .dateTime => '$value.toUtc().toIso8601String()',
        .bytes || .binary => _bytes(value),
        _ => '$value.toJson()',
      },
      ListType(:final item) =>
        '[for (final e$depth in $value) ${encodeNullable(item, 'e$depth', depth + 1)}]',
      MapType(value: final item) =>
        '$value.map((k$depth, v$depth) => MapEntry(k$depth, ${encodeNullable(item, 'v$depth', depth + 1)}))',
      final CustomType custom => switch (_converter(custom)) {
        null => '$value.toJson()',
        final converter => 'const $converter().toJson($value)',
      },
      PatchType(value: final inner) =>
        '$value.toJson((p$depth) => ${encode(inner, 'p$depth', depth + 1)})',
      NamedType() => '$value.toJson()',
    };
  }

  /// The JSON of [value], an expression of [type] that may be `null` when
  /// the type is nullable: `null` stays `null`.
  String encodeNullable(DartType type, String value, [int depth = 0]) {
    final code = encode(type, value, depth);
    if (!type.nullable || isIdentity(type)) return code;
    if (code.startsWith('$value.')) {
      return '$value?.${code.substring(value.length + 1)}';
    }
    return '$value == null ? null : $code';
  }

  /// Whether `==` of [type] compares by identity or shallowly, so a field
  /// of it needs `DeepEquality`: lists, maps, bytes, `JsonValue`, patches.
  bool needsDeep(DartType type) => switch (type) {
    CoreType(:final kind) => switch (kind) {
      .bytes || .binary || .json => true,
      _ => false,
    },
    ListType() || MapType() || PatchType() => true,
    NamedType() || CustomType() => false,
  };

  /// The pattern a `switch` on a JSON value uses for a variant of [type],
  /// such as `String()`; null for a type that takes any JSON.
  String? jsonPattern(DartType type) => switch (type) {
    CoreType(:final kind) => switch (kind) {
      .string || .dateTime || .date || .bytes || .binary => 'String',
      .integer => 'int',
      .float || .number => 'num',
      .boolean => 'bool',
      .json => null,
    },
    ListType() => 'List<Object?>',
    MapType() => 'Map<String, Object?>',
    CustomType(:final json) => jsonPattern(json),
    PatchType(:final value) => jsonPattern(value),
    NamedType(:final id) => switch (declaration(id)) {
      ModelDecl() => 'Map<String, Object?>',
      EnumDecl(:final kind) => switch (kind) {
        .string => 'String',
        .integer => 'int',
        .number => 'num',
        .boolean => 'bool',
      },
      UnionDecl(:final variants) => jsonPattern(variants.first.type),
    },
  };

  /// The converter of [custom], imported from the custom type's library.
  String? _converter(CustomType custom) {
    final converter = custom.converter;
    if (converter != null) _imports.show(custom.import, converter);
    return converter;
  }

  String _bytes(String value) {
    _imports.show('dart:convert', 'base64Encode');
    return 'base64Encode($value)';
  }

  String _runtime(String name, String code) {
    _imports.fromRuntime(name);
    return code;
  }

  String _named(DeclId id, String code) {
    _imports.file(_fileOf(id));
    return code;
  }

  static bool _isJson(DartType type) =>
      type is CoreType && type.kind == CoreKind.json;

  void _use(DartType type) {
    switch (type) {
      case CoreType(:final kind):
        switch (kind) {
          case .date:
            _imports.fromRuntime('Date');
          case .json:
            _imports.fromRuntime('JsonValue');
          case .bytes || .binary:
            _imports.show('dart:typed_data', 'Uint8List');
          default:
        }
      case ListType(:final item):
        _use(item);
      case MapType(:final value):
        _use(value);
      case PatchType(:final value):
        _imports.fromRuntime('Patch');
        _use(value);
      case NamedType(:final id):
        _imports.file(_fileOf(id));
      case CustomType(:final type, :final import):
        _imports.show(import, type);
    }
  }
}
````

````dart
import '../ir/dart_type.dart';
import '../ir/ir.dart';
import 'dart_code.dart';
import 'dart_writer.dart';
import 'model_emitter.dart';
import 'type_codec.dart';

/// Writes a [UnionDecl] (decisions 5–7 of M8): a `sealed` class (an
/// `abstract interface` one when the union is not exhaustive) whose
/// `fromJson` picks a variant, a class per variant that wraps a value of
/// the variant's type (`PetCat(Cat value)`), and a class for a value no
/// variant takes, which keeps the raw JSON (R9.1).
final class UnionEmitter {
  /// Creates an emitter that writes with [_codec].
  const UnionEmitter(this._codec);

  final TypeCodec _codec;

  static const _code = DartCode();

  /// Writes [decl] to [out].
  void write(UnionDecl decl, DartWriter out) {
    final name = decl.name;
    _codec.imports
      ..fromRuntime('immutable')
      ..fromRuntime('JsonValue');
    out.doc(decl.description);
    if (decl.deprecated) out.line(ModelEmitter.deprecation);
    final modifier = decl.exhaustive ? 'sealed' : 'abstract interface';
    out.block('$modifier class $name', () {
      switch (decl.dispatch) {
        case .discriminator:
          _byDiscriminator(out, decl);
        case .jsonType:
          _byJsonType(out, decl);
        case .structural:
          _byStructure(out, decl);
      }
      out
        ..line()
        ..line('/// The value in JSON.')
        ..line('Object? toJson();');
    });
    for (final variant in decl.variants) {
      out.line();
      _variant(out, decl, variant);
    }
    out.line();
    _unknown(out, decl);
  }

  void _byDiscriminator(DartWriter out, UnionDecl decl) {
    final name = decl.name;
    final property = decl.discriminator!;
    _codec.imports.fromRuntime('JsonReader');
    out
      ..line('/// Decodes a [$name] by its `${_plain(property)}`; a value no')
      ..line('/// variant has is a [${decl.unknownName}].')
      ..block('factory $name.fromJson(Object? json)', () {
        out.line(
          'final r = JsonReader.of(json, schema: ${_code.string(name)});',
        );
        final seen = <String>{};
        final cases = <String>[];
        for (final variant in decl.variants) {
          final values = [
            for (final value in variant.discriminatorValues)
              if (seen.add(value)) _code.string(value),
          ];
          if (values.isEmpty) continue;
          cases.add(
            '${values.join(' || ')} => '
            '${_wrap(variant, _decode(variant.type, 'r.json'))},',
          );
        }
        final fallback = switch (decl.defaultVariant) {
          final type? => decl.variants.firstWhere((v) => v.type == type),
          null => null,
        };
        final otherwise = fallback == null
            ? '${decl.unknownName}._(JsonValue(r.json))'
            : _wrap(fallback, _decode(fallback.type, 'r.json'));
        if (cases.isEmpty) {
          out.line('return $otherwise;');
          return;
        }
        out
          ..line(
            'return switch (r.json[${_code.string(property)}]) {',
          )
          ..indented(() {
            cases.forEach(out.line);
            out.line('_ => $otherwise,');
          })
          ..line('};');
      });
  }

  void _byJsonType(DartWriter out, UnionDecl decl) {
    final name = decl.name;
    out
      ..line('/// Decodes a [$name] by the JSON type of [json]; a value of')
      ..line('/// another type is a [${decl.unknownName}].')
      ..line('factory $name.fromJson(Object? json) => switch (json) {')
      ..indented(() {
        // An integer must meet `int` before `num` takes it.
        final ordered = [
          for (final v in decl.variants)
            if (_codec.jsonPattern(v.type) != 'num') v,
          for (final v in decl.variants)
            if (_codec.jsonPattern(v.type) == 'num') v,
        ];
        for (final variant in ordered) {
          final pattern = _codec.jsonPattern(variant.type)!;
          out.line(
            'final $pattern value => '
            '${_wrap(variant, _decode(variant.type, 'value'))},',
          );
        }
        out.line('_ => ${decl.unknownName}._(JsonValue(json)),');
      })
      ..line('};');
  }

  void _byStructure(DartWriter out, UnionDecl decl) {
    final name = decl.name;
    final oneOf = decl.kind == .oneOf;
    _codec.imports.fromRuntime('JsonReader');
    out
      ..line(
        oneOf
            ? '/// Decodes a [$name] as the one variant [json] matches; none or'
            : '/// Decodes a [$name] as the first variant [json] matches; none',
      )
      ..line(
        oneOf
            ? '/// several make a [${decl.unknownName}].'
            : '/// makes a [${decl.unknownName}].',
      )
      ..block('factory $name.fromJson(Object? json)', () {
        out.list('final decoders = <$name Function()>[', [
          for (final variant in decl.variants)
            '() => ${_wrap(variant, _decode(variant.type, 'json'))}',
        ], '];');
        if (oneOf) {
          out
            ..line('final matches = [')
            ..indented(() {
              out.line(
                'for (final decode in decoders) ?JsonReader.attempt(decode),',
              );
            })
            ..line('];')
            ..line(
              'return matches.length == 1 ? matches.single : '
              '${decl.unknownName}._(JsonValue(json));',
            );
        } else {
          out
            ..block('for (final decode in decoders)', () {
              out.line(
                'if (JsonReader.attempt(decode) case final value?) return value;',
              );
            })
            ..line('return ${decl.unknownName}._(JsonValue(json));');
        }
      });
  }

  /// The value of [type] decoded from [json], a JSON expression.
  String _decode(DartType type, String json) => switch (type) {
    NamedType(:final id) when _codec.declaration(id) is ModelDecl =>
      '${_codec.type(type)}.fromJson(JsonReader.asObject($json))',
    _ => '${_codec.decoder(type)}($json)',
  };

  String _wrap(UnionVariantIr variant, String value) =>
      '${variant.name}($value)';

  void _variant(DartWriter out, UnionDecl decl, UnionVariantIr variant) {
    final type = _codec.type(variant.type);
    final name = variant.name;
    out
      ..line('/// A [${decl.name}] that is a `${_plain(type)}`.')
      ..line('@immutable')
      ..block('final class $name implements ${decl.name}', () {
        out
          ..line('/// Wraps [value].')
          ..line('const $name(this.value);')
          ..line()
          ..line('/// The value of the variant.')
          ..line('final $type value;')
          ..line()
          ..line('@override')
          ..line('Object? toJson() => ${_toJson(decl, variant)};')
          ..line()
          ..line('@override');
        final deep = _codec.needsDeep(variant.type);
        if (deep) _codec.imports.fromRuntime('DeepEquality');
        out.chain('bool operator ==(Object other) =>', [
          'other is $name',
          if (deep)
            'const DeepEquality().equals(other.value, value)'
          else
            'other.value == value',
        ], '&&');
        out
          ..line()
          ..line('@override')
          ..line(
            deep
                ? 'int get hashCode => const DeepEquality().hash(value);'
                : 'int get hashCode => value.hashCode;',
          )
          ..line()
          ..line('@override')
          ..line(
            "String toString() => '${name.replaceAll(r'$', r'\$')}"
            r"($value)';",
          );
      });
  }

  /// The JSON of a variant: the value's, and for a model of a union with a
  /// discriminator that the model has no property for, the discriminator
  /// too (decision 5).
  String _toJson(UnionDecl decl, UnionVariantIr variant) {
    final encoded = _codec.encode(variant.type, 'value');
    final property = decl.discriminator;
    final value = variant.discriminatorValues.firstOrNull;
    if (property == null || value == null) return encoded;
    final type = variant.type;
    if (type is! NamedType) return encoded;
    final model = _codec.declaration(type.id);
    if (model is! ModelDecl || model.fields.any((f) => f.jsonKey == property)) {
      return encoded;
    }
    return '{...$encoded, ${_code.string(property)}: ${_code.string(value)}}';
  }

  void _unknown(DartWriter out, UnionDecl decl) {
    final name = decl.unknownName;
    _codec.imports.fromRuntime('DeepEquality');
    out
      ..line('/// A [${decl.name}] that no variant takes; only')
      ..line('/// [${decl.name}.fromJson] creates one.')
      ..line('@immutable')
      ..block('final class $name implements ${decl.name}', () {
        out
          ..line('const $name._(this.json);')
          ..line()
          ..line('/// The JSON as received.')
          ..line('final JsonValue json;')
          ..line()
          ..line('@override')
          ..line('Object? toJson() => json;')
          ..line()
          ..line('@override')
          ..chain('bool operator ==(Object other) =>', [
            'other is $name',
            'const DeepEquality().equals(other.json, json)',
          ], '&&')
          ..line()
          ..line('@override')
          ..line('int get hashCode => const DeepEquality().hash(json);')
          ..line()
          ..line('@override')
          ..line(
            "String toString() => '${name.replaceAll(r'$', r'\$')}"
            r"($json)';",
          );
      });
  }

  /// [text] for a code span in a doc comment: without backticks.
  static String _plain(String text) => text.replaceAll('`', "'");
}
````

- Produces: Фікстури `test/emit/fixtures/pets.yaml`, `everything.yaml`; golden-файли.

R13.2 і R15.3 (частково). Golden: вигадана специфікація `pets.yaml` (модель з enum, union з дискримінатором, union за JSON-типом, тіло PATCH) → файли порівнюються з `test/emit/goldens/pets/**.golden` (розширення `.golden`, щоб `dart analyze packages` їх не читав; `UPDATE_GOLDENS=1 dart test test/emit/generated_code_test.dart` переписує їх). Аналізатор: з `pets.yaml` і змагальної `everything.yaml` (ключові слова, `$`, не-ASCII, рекурсія, порожня модель, лише `additionalProperties`, `Patch`, `unknown`, union-и всіх видів) в обох режимах `union` збирається пакет у тимчасовій теці з runtime за шляхом, `dart pub get --offline` і `dart analyze --fatal-infos` під `package:lints/recommended` плюс правила, які додає `flutter_lints` (переліком: пакет `flutter_lints` не потрібен офлайн); жодного `ignore_for_file`. Декодування: `bin/decode.dart` у тому самому пакеті декодує JSON з невідомим enum, невідомим варіантом, датами, байтами й зайвими ключами, кодує назад і друкує помилки декодування з pointer-ами; тест порівнює stdout.

````dart
@TestOn('vm')
library;

import 'dart:io';

import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/config/generation_config.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/emit/model_library.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/ir/ir_builder.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/overrides/override_set.dart';

import '../helpers/ir_fixture.dart';
import '../helpers/temp_dir.dart';

/// The model files of the fixture spec [name] in `test/emit/fixtures`.
Map<String, String> emitFixture(
  String name, {
  GenerationConfig generation = const GenerationConfig(),
}) {
  final fixture = IrFixture(
    File(p.join('test', 'emit', 'fixtures', name)).readAsStringSync(),
    generation: generation,
  );
  final ir = IrBuilder(
    fixture.diagnostics,
  ).build(fixture.context.spec, fixture.context.client);
  return const ModelLibrary().files(ir);
}

/// Runs `dart` with [arguments] in [directory].
ProcessResult dart(List<String> arguments, String directory) => Process.runSync(
  Platform.resolvedExecutable,
  arguments,
  workingDirectory: directory,
);

void main() {
  test('the pets fixture matches its goldens (R15.3)', () {
    final files = emitFixture('pets.yaml');
    final goldens = p.join('test', 'emit', 'goldens', 'pets');
    if (Platform.environment['UPDATE_GOLDENS'] == '1') {
      for (final MapEntry(:key, :value) in files.entries) {
        File(p.join(goldens, '$key.golden'))
          ..createSync(recursive: true)
          ..writeAsStringSync(value);
      }
    }
    final expected = {
      for (final file in Directory(
        goldens,
      ).listSync(recursive: true).whereType<File>())
        p
            .relative(file.path, from: goldens)
            .replaceAll(r'\', '/')
            .replaceAll(RegExp(r'\.golden$'), ''): file
            .readAsStringSync(),
    };
    expect(files.keys.toSet(), expected.keys.toSet());
    for (final MapEntry(:key, :value) in files.entries) {
      expect(value, expected[key], reason: key);
    }
  });

  group('a package of generated code', () {
    late String package;

    setUpAll(() {
      final temp = TempDir();
      package = temp.path;
      final runtime = p.normalize(
        p.absolute(p.join('..', 'typed_openapi_runtime')),
      );
      temp
        ..write('pubspec.yaml', '''
name: fixture
publish_to: none
environment:
  sdk: ">=3.10.0 <4.0.0"
dependencies:
  typed_openapi_runtime:
    path: ${runtime.replaceAll(r'\', '/')}
dev_dependencies:
  lints: ^6.1.0
''')
        // package:lints/recommended and the rules flutter_lints adds.
        ..write('analysis_options.yaml', '''
include: package:lints/recommended.yaml
linter:
  rules:
    - avoid_print
    - avoid_unnecessary_containers
    - avoid_web_libraries_in_flutter
    - no_logic_in_create_state
    - prefer_const_constructors_in_immutables
    - sized_box_for_whitespace
    - sort_child_properties_last
    - use_build_context_synchronously
    - use_full_hex_values_for_flutter_colors
    - use_key_in_widget_constructors
''')
        ..write('bin/decode.dart', _decodeProgram);
      for (final fixture in ['pets', 'everything']) {
        for (final mode in UnionMode.values) {
          final files = emitFixture(
            '$fixture.yaml',
            generation: GenerationConfig(union: mode),
          );
          for (final MapEntry(:key, :value) in files.entries) {
            temp.write(p.join('lib', '${fixture}_${mode.name}', key), value);
          }
        }
      }
      final get = dart(['pub', 'get', '--offline'], package);
      expect(get.exitCode, 0, reason: '${get.stdout}${get.stderr}');
    });

    test('passes dart analyze with the recommended lints (R13.2)', () {
      final result = dart(['analyze', '--fatal-infos'], package);
      expect(result.exitCode, 0, reason: '${result.stdout}');
    });

    test('decodes and encodes JSON (R15.3)', () {
      final result = dart(['run', 'bin/decode.dart'], package);
      expect(result.stderr, '');
      expect(result.stdout, _decoded);
    });
  }, timeout: const Timeout(Duration(minutes: 3)));
}

const _decodeProgram = r'''
// ignore_for_file: avoid_print
import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:fixture/pets_raw/models.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_runtime/typed_openapi_runtime.dart';

void main() {
  const text = """
{"id": "o-1", "status": "away", "birthday": "2020-02-29",
 "pets": [
   {"kind": "cat", "lives": 7, "bornAt": "2024-01-02T03:04:05+02:00"},
   {"kind": "dog", "bark": false, "photo": "AQID"},
   {"kind": "fish", "fins": 2}
 ],
 "score": 10}
""";
  final owner = Owner.fromJson(jsonDecode(text) as Map<String, Object?>);
  print(jsonEncode(owner.toJson()));
  print(owner.id is IdOrNameString);
  print(owner.status is StatusUnknown);
  final pets = owner.pets!;
  print([for (final pet in pets) pet.runtimeType].join(', '));
  print(owner == Owner.fromJson(jsonDecode(text) as Map<String, Object?>));
  print(owner.hashCode == Owner.fromJson(jsonDecode(text) as Map<String, Object?>).hashCode);
  print(jsonEncode(Owner(id: const IdOrNameInt(5), status: Status.active).toJson()));
  print(Owner.fromJson({'id': true}).id);
  for (final bad in ['{}', '{"id": 1, "status": 1}', '{"id": 1, "pets": [{"kind": "cat"}]}', '{"id": 1, "score": "x"}']) {
    try {
      Owner.fromJson(jsonDecode(bad) as Map<String, Object?>);
    } on JsonDecodeException catch (e) {
      print('${e.pointer} ${e.schema} ${e.message}');
    }
  }
  print(jsonEncode(const PatchOwnerRequest(name: Patch.nullValue()).toJson()));
  print(jsonEncode(const PatchOwnerRequest(meta: Patch.value(JsonValue([1]))).toJson()));
  print(jsonEncode(const PatchOwnerRequest().toJson()));
}
''';

const _decoded = '''
{"id":"o-1","status":"away","birthday":"2020-02-29","pets":[{"kind":"cat","lives":7,"bornAt":"2024-01-02T01:04:05.000Z"},{"bark":false,"photo":"AQID","kind":"dog"},{"kind":"fish","fins":2}],"score":10}
true
true
PetCat, PetDog, PetUnknown
true
true
{"id":5,"status":"active"}
IdOrNameUnknown(true)

{"name":null}
{"meta":[1]}
{}
''';
````

### Task 7: Команда `generate`: тека виходу, маніфест, запобіжники

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/check/spec_checker.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/cli/cli.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/cli/generate_command.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/diagnostics/diagnostic_code.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/generate/output_writer.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/typed_openapi.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/cli/generate_command_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `SpecChecker.check`, `ClientCheck` (M5/M6), `ModelLibrary` (задача 5).
- Produces: `OutputWriter(DiagnosticBag, [DisplayPaths])`: `OutputChanges? write(ClientConfig, Map<String, String> files, {required String inputs, List<String> protected})`, `static String manifestOf(Map<String, String>, {required String inputs})`, `manifest`, `generator`; `OutputChanges` (`written`, `unchanged`, `deleted`); `GenerateCommand(StringSink, [DisplayPaths])`, `static String inputsOf(ClientCheck)`; `ClientCheck.sources`, `ClientCheck.errors`, `ClientCheck.withErrors(int)`; `DiagnosticCode.outputNotOwned` (TOA830), `outputNotWritten` (TOA831), обидва у `fixedCodes`.

Рішення 15–17. `generate [client...]` (`--config/-c`) перевіряє клієнтів, як `check`, пропускає клієнта з помилками після рівнів і baseline (`ClientCheck.errors`; зокрема з непридатним файлом override-ів — рядок M4) і пише моделі в `output` клієнта (ключ є з M1; ширина — `line_width`). `OutputWriter` володіє текою: пише лише змінені файли (тимчасовий файл + rename), видаляє все, чого не згенерував, і порожні теки, останнім пише маніфест `.typed_openapi.json` (генератор із версією, хеш входів, файли з sha256; без часу, тож байт-у-байт). Хеш входів — вміст кожного прочитаного файлу (`ClientCheck.sources`: документи специфікації й файл override-ів) і налаштування клієнта, що змінюють код; шляхи в нього не входять. Запобіжник (TOA830, клієнт пропускається, нічого не пишеться): тека містить `pubspec.yaml`, є `lib/` пакета, містить вхідний файл запуску (конфіг, специфікацію, override-и, baseline) або містить файли без маніфесту. Помилка запису — TOA831. Звіт — як у `check` (текст), потім рядок на клієнта; код виходу — як у `check`. Публічний API: `ModelLibrary`, `OutputWriter`, `OutputChanges`, `ConstraintsIr`, `UnionDispatch`, `UnionMode`.

````dart
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/cli/cli.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/generate/output_writer.dart';

import '../helpers/temp_dir.dart';

const _spec = r'''
openapi: 3.1.0
info: {title: t, version: "1"}
paths:
  /pets:
    get:
      operationId: listPets
      responses:
        '200':
          description: ok
          content:
            application/json:
              schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Pet'}
components:
  schemas:
    Pet:
      type: object
      properties:
        status: {type: string, enum: [a, b]}
''';

void main() {
  late TempDir dir;

  setUp(() {
    dir = TempDir();
    dir.write('specs/pets.yaml', _spec);
  });

  Future<(int, String)> generate(String clients) async {
    dir.write('typed_openapi.yaml', 'clients:\n$clients');
    final out = StringBuffer();
    final code = await TypedOpenApiCli(
      out: out,
      err: out,
      workingDirectory: dir.path,
    ).run(['generate']);
    return (code, out.toString());
  }

  /// The files under [relative] with their content, by relative path.
  Map<String, String> tree(String relative) {
    final root = Directory(p.join(dir.path, relative));
    if (!root.existsSync()) return const {};
    return {
      for (final file
          in root.listSync(recursive: true).whereType<File>().toList()
            ..sort((a, b) => a.path.compareTo(b.path)))
        p.relative(file.path, from: root.path).replaceAll(r'\', '/'): file
            .readAsStringSync(),
    };
  }

  test('writes the models and the manifest, then nothing new', () async {
    const client = '  pets: {input: specs/pets.yaml, output: gen/pets}\n';
    final (code, out) = await generate(client);
    expect(code, 0);
    expect(
      out,
      endsWith(
        'Generated pets in ${p.join('gen', 'pets')}: 3 files, 3 written, '
        '0 deleted.\n',
      ),
    );
    final first = tree('gen/pets');
    expect(first.keys, [
      '.typed_openapi.json',
      'models.dart',
      'models/pet.dart',
      'models/pet_status.dart',
    ]);
    final manifest =
        jsonDecode(first['.typed_openapi.json']!) as Map<String, Object?>;
    expect(manifest['generator'], OutputWriter.generator);
    expect(manifest['inputs'], hasLength(64));
    expect((manifest['files']! as Map<String, Object?>).keys, [
      'models.dart',
      'models/pet.dart',
      'models/pet_status.dart',
    ]);
    final (again, log) = await generate(client);
    expect(again, 0);
    expect(log, contains('3 files, 0 written, 0 deleted.'));
    expect(tree('gen/pets'), first);
  });

  test('deletes what it no longer writes, empty directories too', () async {
    const client = '  pets: {input: specs/pets.yaml, output: gen/pets}\n';
    await generate(client);
    dir
      ..write('gen/pets/models/old.dart', '// old')
      ..write('gen/pets/stale/deep/x.dart', '// x');
    final (code, out) = await generate(client);
    expect(code, 0);
    expect(out, contains('3 files, 0 written, 2 deleted.'));
    expect(tree('gen/pets').keys, [
      '.typed_openapi.json',
      'models.dart',
      'models/pet.dart',
      'models/pet_status.dart',
    ]);
    expect(Directory(p.join(dir.path, 'gen/pets/stale')).existsSync(), isFalse);
  });

  test('the manifest changes with the inputs', () async {
    const client = '  pets: {input: specs/pets.yaml, output: gen/pets}\n';
    await generate(client);
    final before = tree('gen/pets')['.typed_openapi.json'];
    dir.write('specs/pets.yaml', '$_spec\n# a comment\n');
    await generate(client);
    expect(tree('gen/pets')['.typed_openapi.json'], isNot(before));
  });

  test('skips a client whose check reports errors', () async {
    final (code, out) = await generate(
      '  pets: {input: specs/pets.yaml, output: gen/pets, '
      'overrides: missing.yaml}\n',
    );
    expect(code, 1);
    expect(out, contains('error TOA801 overrides-not-found'));
    expect(
      out,
      endsWith(
        'Skipped pets: its check reports errors; fix them and run generate '
        'again.\n',
      ),
    );
    expect(tree('gen/pets'), isEmpty);
  });

  test('the manifest names the version of the pubspec', () {
    final version = RegExp(
      r'^version: (\S+)$',
      multiLine: true,
    ).firstMatch(File('pubspec.yaml').readAsStringSync())![1];
    expect(OutputWriter.generator, 'typed_openapi $version');
  });

  group('refuses an output directory it may not own (TOA830)', () {
    Future<void> refused(String output, String why) async {
      final (code, out) = await generate(
        '  pets: {input: specs/pets.yaml, output: $output}\n',
      );
      expect(code, 1);
      expect(out, contains('error TOA830 output-not-owned'));
      expect(out, contains(why));
      expect(out, contains('Skipped pets: see the error above.'));
    }

    test('a package', () async {
      dir.write('app/pubspec.yaml', 'name: app\n');
      await refused('app', 'holds a pubspec.yaml');
      expect(tree('app').keys, ['pubspec.yaml']);
    });

    test("a package's lib", () async {
      dir
        ..write('app/pubspec.yaml', 'name: app\n')
        ..write('app/lib/main.dart', '// mine');
      await refused('app/lib', 'is the lib directory of a package');
      expect(tree('app/lib').keys, ['main.dart']);
    });

    test('a directory with an input of the run', () async {
      await refused('specs', 'an input of the run');
      expect(tree('specs').keys, ['pets.yaml']);
    });

    test('a directory with files of someone else', () async {
      dir.write('gen/pets/notes.txt', 'mine');
      await refused('gen/pets', 'holds files but no .typed_openapi.json');
      expect(tree('gen/pets').keys, ['notes.txt']);
    });
  });
}
````

Файл `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/cli/generate_command.dart` (новий):

````dart
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:args/command_runner.dart';
import 'package:crypto/crypto.dart';
import 'package:io/io.dart' show ExitCode;

import '../check/spec_checker.dart';
import '../config/config_loader.dart';
import '../diagnostics/diagnostic_bag.dart';
import '../diagnostics/display_paths.dart';
import '../diagnostics/reporters.dart';
import '../emit/model_library.dart';
import '../generate/output_writer.dart';
import 'check_command.dart';

/// `typed_openapi generate`: checks every client as `check` does and
/// writes the models of each client without errors into its output
/// directory (M8; operations come with M9).
///
/// A client whose check reports an error, including an overrides file that
/// cannot be used, is skipped; so is one whose output directory the
/// generator may not own (TOA830). The report and a line per client go to
/// stdout, and the exit code is the one `check` gives.
final class GenerateCommand extends Command<int> {
  /// Creates the command; reports go to [_out], and files are named as
  /// [_paths] does.
  GenerateCommand(this._out, [this._paths = const DisplayPaths()]) {
    argParser.addOption(
      'config',
      abbr: 'c',
      defaultsTo: 'typed_openapi.yaml',
      help: 'Path to the config file.',
    );
  }

  final StringSink _out;
  final DisplayPaths _paths;

  @override
  String get name => 'generate';

  @override
  String get description =>
      'Check specs and generate the models of clients without errors.';

  @override
  String get invocation =>
      '${runner!.executableName} generate [options] [client...]';

  @override
  Future<int> run() async {
    final results = argResults!;
    final diagnostics = DiagnosticBag();
    final configPath = results.option('config')!;
    final config = ConfigLoader(diagnostics, _paths).load(configPath);
    final lines = <String>[];
    if (config != null) {
      final only = results.rest.toSet();
      final unknown = only.difference(config.declaredClients);
      if (unknown.isNotEmpty) {
        usageException(
          'Unknown client: ${(unknown.toList()..sort()).join(', ')}.',
        );
      }
      final checks = SpecChecker(diagnostics, _paths).check(config, only: only);
      final writer = OutputWriter(diagnostics, _paths);
      for (final check in checks) {
        final client = check.client;
        final ir = check.ir;
        if (ir == null || check.errors > 0) {
          lines.add(
            'Skipped ${client.name}: its check reports errors; fix them and '
            'run generate again.',
          );
          continue;
        }
        final files = ModelLibrary(width: client.lineWidth).files(ir);
        final changes = writer.write(
          client,
          files,
          inputs: inputsOf(check),
          protected: [
            _paths.absolute(configPath),
            ...check.sources,
            ?client.baseline,
          ],
        );
        lines.add(
          changes == null
              ? 'Skipped ${client.name}: see the error above.'
              : 'Generated ${client.name} in ${_paths.of(client.output)}: '
                    '${files.length} files, ${changes.written} written, '
                    '${changes.deleted} deleted.',
        );
      }
    }
    _out.writeln(const TextReporter().format(diagnostics));
    lines.forEach(_out.writeln);
    if (config == null) return ExitCode.config.code;
    return diagnostics.hasErrors
        ? CheckCommand.exitHasErrors
        : ExitCode.success.code;
  }

  /// The hash of what the code of [check]'s client is generated from: the
  /// content of every file the run read, the client's settings that change
  /// the code, and the generator. The same inputs give the same hash on
  /// any machine: paths are not part of it.
  static String inputsOf(ClientCheck check) {
    final client = check.client;
    String hashOf(String path) {
      try {
        return sha256.convert(File(path).readAsBytesSync()).toString();
      } on FileSystemException {
        return 'unreadable';
      }
    }

    return sha256
        .convert(
          utf8.encode(
            jsonEncode({
              'generator': OutputWriter.generator,
              'files': [for (final path in check.sources) hashOf(path)],
              'generation': client.generation.key,
              'line_width': client.lineWidth,
              'preset': client.preset?.name,
              'rules': client.disabledRules.toList()..sort(),
            }),
          ),
        )
        .toString();
  }
}
````

Файл `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/generate/output_writer.dart` (новий):

````dart
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:crypto/crypto.dart';
import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;

import '../config/project_config.dart';
import '../diagnostics/diagnostic_bag.dart';
import '../diagnostics/display_paths.dart';

/// What [OutputWriter.write] did to an output directory.
final class OutputChanges {
  /// Creates the counts.
  const OutputChanges({
    required this.written,
    required this.unchanged,
    required this.deleted,
  });

  /// Files written because they are new or their content changed.
  final int written;

  /// Files whose content was already the generated one.
  final int unchanged;

  /// Files deleted because the generator no longer writes them.
  final int deleted;
}

/// Writes the generated files of a client into its output directory, which
/// the generator owns (decisions 15 and 17 of M8).
///
/// A file is written only when its content changes, through a temporary
/// file that is renamed over it, so a reader never sees half a file; every
/// file the generator did not write this time is deleted, and so are
/// directories left empty. The manifest [manifest] lists the generator, a
/// hash of the inputs and every file with its hash; it is written last.
///
/// The directory is refused (TOA830, nothing is written) when it holds a
/// `pubspec.yaml`, is the `lib` directory of a package, holds an input of
/// the run (the config, the spec, the overrides or the baseline file), or
/// holds files but no manifest: files the generator did not write.
final class OutputWriter {
  /// Creates a writer that reports to [_diagnostics]; [_paths] names
  /// directories in diagnostics.
  const OutputWriter(this._diagnostics, [this._paths = const DisplayPaths()]);

  /// The name of the manifest in the output directory.
  static const manifest = '.typed_openapi.json';

  /// The generator and its version, as the manifest names them.
  static const generator = 'typed_openapi 0.0.1';

  final DiagnosticBag _diagnostics;
  final DisplayPaths _paths;

  /// Writes [files] (paths relative to the output directory of [client])
  /// and the manifest with [inputs], the hash of what they were generated
  /// from; [protected] are the input files of the run, which the directory
  /// must not hold. Returns null after reporting why nothing, or not
  /// everything, was written.
  OutputChanges? write(
    ClientConfig client,
    Map<String, String> files, {
    required String inputs,
    List<String> protected = const [],
  }) {
    final root = p.normalize(p.absolute(client.output));
    if (!_owned(client, root, protected)) return null;
    final sorted = files.keys.toList()..sort();
    var written = 0;
    var unchanged = 0;
    var deleted = 0;
    try {
      for (final path in sorted) {
        if (_writeFile(p.join(root, path), files[path]!)) {
          written++;
        } else {
          unchanged++;
        }
      }
      final keep = {
        ...sorted.map((f) => p.join(root, f)),
        p.join(root, manifest),
      };
      final directory = Directory(root);
      final entries = directory.listSync(recursive: true, followLinks: false)
        ..sort((a, b) => b.path.compareTo(a.path));
      for (final entry in entries) {
        if (entry is Directory) {
          if (entry.listSync().isEmpty) entry.deleteSync();
        } else if (!keep.contains(entry.path)) {
          entry.deleteSync();
          deleted++;
        }
      }
      _writeFile(p.join(root, manifest), manifestOf(files, inputs: inputs));
    } on FileSystemException catch (e) {
      _diagnostics.report(
        .outputNotWritten,
        'Cannot write the output of client "${client.name}" to '
        '${_paths.of(root)}: ${e.message}${e.path == null ? '' : ' (${_paths.of(e.path!)})'}.',
        file: _paths.of(root),
        fix:
            'Check that the directory can be written, then run generate '
            'again.',
      );
      return null;
    }
    return OutputChanges(
      written: written,
      unchanged: unchanged,
      deleted: deleted,
    );
  }

  /// The manifest of [files] generated from [inputs]: JSON with sorted
  /// keys, the same for the same files.
  static String manifestOf(
    Map<String, String> files, {
    required String inputs,
  }) {
    final hashes = {
      for (final path in files.keys.toList()..sort())
        path: sha256.convert(utf8.encode(files[path]!)).toString(),
    };
    return '${const JsonEncoder.withIndent('  ').convert({
      'generator': generator,
      'inputs': inputs,
      'files': hashes,
    })}\n';
  }

  /// Whether the generator may own [root]; reports why not.
  bool _owned(ClientConfig client, String root, List<String> protected) {
    void refuse(String why, String fix) => _diagnostics.report(
      .outputNotOwned,
      'Client "${client.name}" is not generated: its output directory '
      '${_paths.of(root)} $why. The generator deletes every file there it '
      'did not write.',
      file: _paths.of(root),
      fix: fix,
    );
    const move =
        'Point output to a directory of its own, such as '
        'lib/src/<client>.';
    final type = FileSystemEntity.typeSync(root, followLinks: false);
    if (type == FileSystemEntityType.notFound) return true;
    if (type != FileSystemEntityType.directory) {
      refuse('is not a directory', move);
      return false;
    }
    if (File(p.join(root, 'pubspec.yaml')).existsSync()) {
      refuse('holds a pubspec.yaml', move);
      return false;
    }
    if (p.basename(root) == 'lib' &&
        File(p.join(p.dirname(root), 'pubspec.yaml')).existsSync()) {
      refuse('is the lib directory of a package', move);
      return false;
    }
    for (final input in protected) {
      final path = p.normalize(p.absolute(input));
      if (p.isWithin(root, path)) {
        refuse('holds ${_paths.of(path)}, an input of the run', move);
        return false;
      }
    }
    final entries = Directory(root).listSync();
    if (entries.isNotEmpty && !File(p.join(root, manifest)).existsSync()) {
      refuse(
        'holds files but no $manifest, so they were not written by it',
        'Empty the directory, or point output to a new one.',
      );
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  /// Writes [content] to [path] through a temporary file, unless the file
  /// has it already; returns whether it was written.
  static bool _writeFile(String path, String content) {
    final file = File(path);
    if (file.existsSync() && file.readAsStringSync() == content) return false;
    file.parent.createSync(recursive: true);
    final temp = File('$path.typed_openapi.tmp');
    try {
      temp.writeAsStringSync(content, flush: true);
      temp.renameSync(path);
    } finally {
      if (temp.existsSync()) temp.deleteSync();
    }
    return true;
  }
}
````

### Task 8: Корпус, fuzz, виправлення і документація

**Files:**
- Modify: `CLAUDE.md`
- Modify: `docs/roadmap.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/CHANGELOG.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/README.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/model_emitter.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/type_codec.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/union_emitter.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/schema_mapper.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/fixtures/everything.yaml`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/model_library_test.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/ir/union_dispatch_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Задачі 1–7.
- Produces: `TypeCodec.decodeValue(DartType, String json)`; `_Pending.jsonKind` у `SchemaMapper` (внутрішнє).

Прогін `generate` на 14 клієнтах корпусу (вихід — у scratchpad, `dart analyze` на кожному пакеті) і fuzz-харнес M6 (емісія кожної IR, вибірково — аналізатор) знайшли три дефекти, які тут виправлено з регресійними тестами:

1. Обов'язкове nullable-поле, чиє кодування — не ланцюжок (`List<DateTime>?`): `x == null ? null : [for (... in x) ...]` не компілюється, бо поле не просувається (`unchecked_use_of_nullable_value`, 13 помилок в airflow, discord, openai). Тепер — `switch (x) {final v? => ..., null => null}`.
2. Варіант union-а, що став типом «будь-який JSON» (виключена override-ом схема, рекурсивний аліас), при диспетчеризації за JSON-типом обвалював емітер (`null`-патерн). `SchemaMapper` тепер перевіряє й типи варіантів після мапінгу: такий union стає structural з TOA453; вкладений union без дискримінатора не вважається об'єктом.
3. Варіант власного типу в union-і викликав лямбду-декодер некоректно (`(value) => X.fromJson(value)(json)`); `TypeCodec.decodeValue` пише виклик напряму.

Документація: `CLAUDE.md` (`emit/`, `generate/`, `GenerateCommand`, runtime), `CHANGELOG.md`, `README.md`, розділ «Перенесено з M8» у roadmap.

````diff
 ## 0.0.1 - Unreleased
 
 ### Added
+- Models (M8): `generate` writes the models of every client without check
+  errors into its `output` directory: a file per model, enum and union in
+  `models/` and the barrel `models.dart`, all starting with
+  `// dart format off` and clean under `package:lints/recommended` and
+  `flutter_lints`. Models are `final` and `@immutable`, with `fromJson`,
+  `toJson`, deep `==`/`hashCode` and `toString`; a `const` property is a
+  constant getter, `additionalProperties` a map field, constraints, defaults
+  and descriptions doc comments, `deprecated` `@Deprecated`. Enums are open
+  (`Status`, `StatusKnown`, `StatusUnknown`); request models take only known
+  values. Unions wrap their variants (`PetCat(Cat value)`) and pick one by
+  the discriminator, by the JSON type, or, with `union: structural` (config
+  key or schema override), by trying them; a union whose variants cannot be
+  told apart is `JsonValue` (TOA453). The output directory is owned by the
+  generator: files it did not write are deleted, a directory that holds a
+  package, an input or foreign files is refused (TOA830), and
+  `.typed_openapi.json` lists the generator, a hash of the inputs and the
+  files.
 - Semantic IR (M6): `check` builds each client's IR (`IrBuilder`,
   `ClientIr`) from the normalized spec and reports naming and mapping
   problems. Types follow R6.2 (`integer` -> `int`, `number` -> `num` or
````

### Task 9: Рішення користувача: поліморфізм `allOf`, часткова диспетчеризація, аліаси дискримінатора, секрети

**Files:**
- Modify: `CLAUDE.md`
- Modify: `docs/roadmap.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/CHANGELOG.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/diagnostics/diagnostic_code.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/model_emitter.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/type_codec.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/union_emitter.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/ir.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/schema_mapper.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/fixtures/pets.yaml`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/generated_code_test.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/goldens/pets/models/owner.dart.golden`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/goldens/pets/models/pet.dart.golden`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/model_library_test.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/ir/polymorphism_test.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/ir/schema_mapper_semantics_test.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/ir/union_dispatch_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Задачі 2, 3, 5, 8: `SchemaMapper._declare`, `_union`, `_component`, `_conflicts`; `UnionEmitter`, `ModelEmitter`, `TypeCodec`.
- Produces: `enum JsonKind { string, integer, number, boolean, array, object }`; `UnionVariantIr.jsonKinds`; `FieldIr.sensitive`; `DiagnosticCode.discriminatorWithoutSubtypes` (TOA452, замість `inheritancePolymorphismDeferred`); у згенерованому коді — поле `discriminator` і конструктор `_` обгорток, обгортка `<Union>Json`.

Відповіді на відкриті питання першої версії плану (розділ «Рішення користувача»; ухвалені рішення 14–17).

- **Поліморфізм `allOf`.** `SchemaMapper._subtypes` знаходить компоненти, що розширюють базу з дискримінатором (транзитивно, у порядку `components.schemas`, без виключених override-ом); `_declare` робить з такої бази union (`_union(..., subtypes: true)`: варіанти — `_component` підтипів, значення — `mapping` або ім'я компонента, `defaultMapping` як у union-ів). Значення, що веде на саму базу, — TOA451. `_component` не згортає `allOf: [$ref: База]` у посилання на базу. Дискримінатор, який нічого не вибирає, — TOA452 (`discriminatorWithoutSubtypes`, перейменовано); у `_merged` TOA452 більше не звітується. Емітер друкує такі union-и тим самим шляхом, що й `oneOf` з дискримінатором.
- **Часткова диспетчеризація.** `_conflicts` повертає гілки без власного JSON-типу; `_isRawUnion` робить `JsonValue` лише union, у якому таких гілок стільки, скільки гілок узагалі (або є гілка «будь-який JSON»). Інакше `_union` мапить лише гілки з власним типом, а решту збирає в один варіант `CoreType(.json)` з `UnionVariantIr.jsonKinds` (публічний `JsonKind`) і звітує TOA453 з іменами цих гілок. `UnionEmitter` пише для такого варіанта по рядку на JSON-тип (`final Map<String, Object?> value => PetJson(JsonValue(value))`), `num` — після `int`.
- **Аліаси дискримінатора.** Обгортка моделі без властивості-дискримінатора має поле `discriminator`, приватний конструктор `_` для `fromJson` (`('dog' || 'puppy') && final String tag => PetDog._(..., tag)`), `toJson` пише `discriminator ?? 'dog'`, `==`/`hashCode` його враховують.
- **Секрети.** `FieldIr.sensitive` (`writeOnly` або `format: password` у властивості, її анотаційних частинах чи цілі `$ref`); `ModelEmitter` друкує `<redacted>`.
- Емітер імпортує `JsonReader` лише туди, де декодер його пише (корпус дав 10 warning-ів `unused_shown_name` на union-ах з enum-варіантом і спільною обгорткою).
- Фікстура `pets.yaml` отримує аліас `puppy` і поле `format: password`; golden-файли `owner`, `pet` і очікуваний вивід декодування оновлено. Документація: `CLAUDE.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`; у roadmap «Перенесено з M8» — відкладені рішення про R5.4 (`typeName`) і `title` const-гілки, Stripe `anyOf: [string, {enum: ['']}]` і `DirectionIndex` для підтипів.

````diff
-  int get hashCode => value.hashCode;
+  int get hashCode => Object.hash(value, discriminator);
 
   @override
   String toString() => 'PetDog($value)';
````

Файл `packages/typed_openapi/test/ir/polymorphism_test.dart` (новий):

````dart
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/diagnostics/diagnostic_code.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/ir/ir.dart';

import '../helpers/ir_fixture.dart';
import '../helpers/mapping.dart';

T declaration<T extends Declaration>(ClientIr ir, String name) =>
    ir.declarations.firstWhere((d) => d.name == name) as T;

/// The variants of the union [name] as `Class [values]` strings.
List<String> variants(ClientIr ir, String name) => [
  for (final v in declaration<UnionDecl>(ir, name).variants)
    '${v.name} ${v.discriminatorValues}',
];

void main() {
  group('a base with a discriminator is a union of its subtypes', () {
    const head = r'''
openapi: 3.1.0
info: {title: t, version: "1"}
components:
  schemas:
    Holder:
      type: object
      properties: {pet: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Pet'}}
''';

    test('over every level of the hierarchy, with implicit values', () {
      final fixture = IrFixture(
        r'''
$head    Pet:
      type: object
      required: [petType]
      properties: {petType: {type: string}, name: {type: string}}
      discriminator: {propertyName: petType}
    Cat:
      allOf:
        - $ref: '#/components/schemas/Pet'
        - {properties: {lives: {type: integer}}}
    Kitten:
      allOf:
        - $ref: '#/components/schemas/Cat'
        - {properties: {age: {type: integer}}}
    Dog:
      allOf: [{$ref: '#/components/schemas/Pet'}]
'''
            .replaceFirst(r'$head', head),
      );
      final (ir, types) = mapComponents(fixture, ['Holder']);
      expect(fixture.codes, isEmpty);
      expect(fieldsOf(ir, 'Holder'), ['pet(pet): Pet?']);
      final pet = declaration<UnionDecl>(ir, 'Pet');
      expect(pet.dispatch, UnionDispatch.discriminator);
      expect(pet.discriminator, 'petType');
      expect(variants(ir, 'Pet'), [
        'PetCat [Cat]',
        'PetKitten [Kitten]',
        'PetDog [Dog]',
      ]);
      expect(fieldsOf(ir, 'Kitten'), [
        'petType(petType): String',
        'name(name): String?',
        'lives(lives): int?',
        'age(age): int?',
      ]);
    });

    test('a mapping to the base itself is reported and decodes as unknown', () {
      final fixture = IrFixture(
        r'''
$head    Pet:
      type: object
      properties: {petType: {type: string}}
      discriminator:
        propertyName: petType
        mapping: {pet: Pet, cat: '#/components/schemas/Cat', bird: Holder}
    Cat:
      allOf: [{$ref: '#/components/schemas/Pet'}]
'''
            .replaceFirst(r'$head', head),
      );
      final (ir, _) = mapComponents(fixture, ['Holder']);
      expect(variants(ir, 'Pet'), ['PetCat [cat]']);
      expect(fixture.codes, [
        (
          DiagnosticCode.discriminatorMappingNotVariant,
          '#/components/schemas/Pet/discriminator/mapping/pet',
        ),
        (
          DiagnosticCode.discriminatorMappingNotVariant,
          '#/components/schemas/Pet/discriminator/mapping/bird',
        ),
      ]);
      expect(fixture.reported.first.message, contains('base schema itself'));
    });

    test('a recursive hierarchy refers to the union from a subtype', () {
      final fixture = IrFixture(
        r'''
$head    Pet:
      type: object
      properties:
        petType: {type: string}
        friends: {type: array, items: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Pet'}}
      discriminator: {propertyName: petType}
    Cat:
      allOf:
        - $ref: '#/components/schemas/Pet'
        - {properties: {mother: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Cat'}}}
'''
            .replaceFirst(r'$head', head),
      );
      final (ir, _) = mapComponents(fixture, ['Holder']);
      expect(fixture.codes, isEmpty);
      expect(fieldsOf(ir, 'Cat'), [
        'petType(petType): String?',
        'friends(friends): List<Pet>?',
        'mother(mother): Cat?',
      ]);
      expect(variants(ir, 'Pet'), ['PetCat [Cat]']);
    });

    test('a subtype that is a base itself is its own union too', () {
      final fixture = IrFixture(
        r'''
$head    Pet:
      type: object
      properties: {petType: {type: string}}
      discriminator: {propertyName: petType}
    Cat:
      allOf: [{$ref: '#/components/schemas/Pet'}]
      discriminator: {propertyName: petType}
    Kitten:
      allOf: [{$ref: '#/components/schemas/Cat'}]
'''
            .replaceFirst(r'$head', head),
      );
      final (ir, _) = mapComponents(fixture, ['Holder']);
      expect(variants(ir, 'Pet'), ['PetCat [Cat]', 'PetKitten [Kitten]']);
      expect(variants(ir, 'Cat'), ['CatKitten [Kitten]']);
    });

    test('a base used only as an allOf part stays out of the IR', () {
      final fixture = IrFixture(r'''
openapi: 3.1.0
info: {title: t, version: "1"}
components:
  schemas:
    Pet:
      type: object
      properties: {petType: {type: string}}
      discriminator: {propertyName: petType}
    Cat:
      allOf: [{$ref: '#/components/schemas/Pet'}]
''');
      final (ir, _) = mapComponents(fixture, ['Cat']);
      expect(fixture.codes, isEmpty);
      expect(ir.declarations.map((d) => d.name), ['Cat']);
    });

    test('an excluded subtype is left out', () {
      final fixture = IrFixture(
        r'''
$head    Pet:
      type: object
      properties: {petType: {type: string}}
      discriminator: {propertyName: petType}
    Cat:
      allOf: [{$ref: '#/components/schemas/Pet'}]
    Dog:
      allOf: [{$ref: '#/components/schemas/Pet'}]
'''
            .replaceFirst(r'$head', head),
        overrides: 'schemas:\n  Dog: {exclude: true}\n',
      );
      final (ir, _) = mapComponents(fixture, ['Holder']);
      expect(variants(ir, 'Pet'), ['PetCat [Cat]']);
    });
  });

  group('a union without a discriminator dispatches what it can', () {
    test('a shared JSON type is one variant of any JSON value (R9.2)', () {
      final fixture = IrFixture(r'''
openapi: 3.1.0
info: {title: t, version: "1"}
components:
  schemas:
    Customer: {type: object, properties: {id: {type: string}}}
    DeletedCustomer: {type: object, properties: {deleted: {type: boolean}}}
    Holder:
      type: object
      properties:
        customer:
          anyOf:
            - {type: string, maxLength: 5000}
            - $ref: '#/components/schemas/Customer'
            - $ref: '#/components/schemas/DeletedCustomer'
''');
      final (ir, _) = mapComponents(fixture, ['Holder']);
      final customer = declaration<UnionDecl>(ir, 'HolderCustomer');
      expect(customer.dispatch, UnionDispatch.jsonType);
      expect(customer.variants.map((v) => '${v.name} ${v.jsonKinds}'), [
        'HolderCustomerString []',
        'HolderCustomerJson [JsonKind.object]',
      ]);
      expect(ir.declarations.map((d) => d.name), ['Holder', 'HolderCustomer']);
      expect(fixture.codes, [
        (
          DiagnosticCode.unionUndiscriminated,
          '#/components/schemas/Holder/properties/customer',
        ),
      ]);
      final found = fixture.reported.single;
      expect(
        found.message,
        startsWith(
          'Variants Customer, DeletedCustomer of this anyOf share the JSON '
          'type object',
        ),
      );
      expect(found.fix, contains('customer:\n        union: structural'));
    });

    test('several shared types go to one variant at the first of them', () {
      final fixture = IrFixture(r'''
openapi: 3.1.0
info: {title: t, version: "1"}
components:
  schemas:
    A: {type: object, properties: {a: {type: string}}}
    B: {type: object, properties: {b: {type: string}}}
    Mixed:
      oneOf:
        - {type: integer}
        - {type: string}
        - $ref: '#/components/schemas/A'
        - {type: string, format: uuid}
        - $ref: '#/components/schemas/B'
''');
      final (ir, _) = mapComponents(fixture, ['Mixed']);
      final mixed = declaration<UnionDecl>(ir, 'Mixed');
      expect(mixed.variants.map((v) => '${v.name} ${v.jsonKinds}'), [
        'MixedInt []',
        'MixedJson [JsonKind.string, JsonKind.object]',
      ]);
      expect(fixture.reported.single.message, contains('#1, A, #3, B'));
    });

    test('a variant of any JSON value makes the whole union JsonValue', () {
      final fixture = IrFixture('''
openapi: 3.1.0
info: {title: t, version: "1"}
components:
  schemas:
    Loose:
      oneOf: [{}, {type: string}]
''');
      final (_, types) = mapComponents(fixture, ['Loose']);
      expect(types['Loose'], 'JsonValue');
      expect(fixture.codes, [
        (DiagnosticCode.unionUndiscriminated, '#/components/schemas/Loose'),
      ]);
    });
  });
}
````

````diff
+  base itself is TOA451 and decodes as unknown; one that nothing extends
+  is TOA452); a subtype that is only `allOf: [$ref: Base]` stays a model.
+  A wrapper whose model has no discriminator property keeps the value it

+  mapping value. `toString` prints `<redacted>` for `FieldIr.sensitive`
````
